SELECT
    c.id,
    c.potential_board_id,
    c.skill_lv_max,
    c.rarity,
    c.optimal_awakening_grow_type,
    c.passive_skill_set_id,
    c.link_skill1_id,
    c.link_skill2_id,
    c.link_skill3_id,
    c.link_skill4_id,
    c.link_skill5_id,
    c.link_skill6_id,
    c.link_skill7_id,
    oag.step AS oag_step,
    oag.skill_lv_max AS oag_skill_lv_max
FROM cards c
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT o1.*
    FROM optimal_awakening_growths o1
    JOIN (
        SELECT optimal_awakening_grow_type, MAX(step) AS max_step
        FROM optimal_awakening_growths
        WHERE step IN (8, 4, 7, 3)
        GROUP BY optimal_awakening_grow_type
    ) o2 ON o1.optimal_awakening_grow_type = o2.optimal_awakening_grow_type
         AND o1.step = o2.max_step
) oag ON oag.optimal_awakening_grow_type = c.optimal_awakening_grow_type
WHERE (CAST(c.id AS TEXT) LIKE '1%1' OR CAST(c.id AS TEXT) LIKE '4%1' OR CAST(c.id AS TEXT) LIKE '4%2')
  AND LENGTH(CAST(c.id AS TEXT)) <= 9
  AND c.rarity IN (4, 5)
  AND c.lv_max > 119
  AND c.open_at < datetime('now')
  AND c.id <= 481899781
  AND c.id NOT IN (17426891)

UPDATE players SET selected_team_num = 
SELECT json_object(
    'id', c.id,
    'card_id', c.id,
    'exp', 50000000,
    'skill_lv', COALESCE(oag.skill_lv_max, c.skill_lv_max),
    'is_favorite', json('false'),
    'awakening_route_id', 0,
    'is_released_potential', json('true'),
    'released_rate', 100,
    'optimal_awakening_step', COALESCE(oag.step,
        CASE
        WHEN c.rarity = 4 AND c.optimal_awakening_grow_type IS NOT NULL THEN 7
        WHEN c.rarity = 5 AND c.optimal_awakening_grow_type IS NOT NULL THEN 3
        ELSE 0
        END
    ),
    'card_decoration_id', 0,
    'exchangeable_item_id', 0,
    'awakenings', json('[]'),
    'unlocked_square_statuses', json_array(0,0,0,0),
    'updated_at', __TS__,
    'created_at', __TS__,
    'potential_parameters', json_array(
        json_object('parameter_id', null, 'parameter_type', 'Hp', 'total_value', 5000),
        json_object('parameter_id', null, 'parameter_type', 'Atk', 'total_value', 5000),
        json_object('parameter_id', null, 'parameter_type', 'Defense', 'total_value', 5000),
        json_object('parameter_id', 1, 'parameter_type', 'Skill', 'total_value', 15),
        json_object('parameter_id', 2, 'parameter_type', 'Skill', 'total_value', 15),
        json_object('parameter_id', 3, 'parameter_type', 'Skill', 'total_value', 15),
        json_object('parameter_id', 4, 'parameter_type', 'Skill', 'total_value', 15),
        json_object('parameter_id', 5, 'parameter_type', 'Skill', 'total_value', 15),
        json_object('parameter_id', 6, 'parameter_type', 'Skill', 'total_value', 15),
        json_object('parameter_id', 7, 'parameter_type', 'Skill', 'total_value', 15)
    ),
    'equipment_skill_items', json('[]'),
    'link_skill_lvs', (
        SELECT json_group_array(json_object('id', link_id, 'skill_lv', 10))
        FROM (
            SELECT c.link_skill1_id AS link_id UNION ALL
            SELECT c.link_skill2_id UNION ALL
            SELECT c.link_skill3_id UNION ALL
            SELECT c.link_skill4_id UNION ALL
            SELECT c.link_skill5_id UNION ALL
            SELECT c.link_skill6_id UNION ALL
            SELECT c.link_skill7_id
        )
        WHERE link_id IS NOT NULL
    )
) AS result
FROM cards c
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT o1.*
    FROM optimal_awakening_growths o1
    JOIN (
        SELECT optimal_awakening_grow_type, MAX(step) AS max_step
        FROM optimal_awakening_growths
        WHERE step IN (8, 4, 7, 3)
        GROUP BY optimal_awakening_grow_type
    ) o2 ON o1.optimal_awakening_grow_type = o2.optimal_awakening_grow_type
         AND o1.step = o2.max_step
) oag ON oag.optimal_awakening_grow_type = c.optimal_awakening_grow_type
WHERE c.id = __CARD_ID__
LIMIT 1;
